([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-clustering-visualization-of-recovered-barcodes))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when promoter/non-promoter sequences (e.g. the iPro-WAEL 'data' folder human cell-line files) must be encoded with fused sequence descriptors and visually checked for class separation via t-SNE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have a per-cell feature matrix from MIRIAM output (autoencoder shape latent vectors or compartment-quantified marker intensities) and want a 2D embedding to explore clustering of morphologically or phenotypically similar cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have high-dimensional MS2DeepScore embeddings (e.g. 200-dim vectors from 3601 test-set spectra) and need a 2D visualization to check whether spectra group by chemical (super)class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a raw (dropout-containing), noisy, or imputed scRNA-Seq matrix needs a 2-D visualization of cell-type clusters, or when computing average silhouette width (ASW) on the t-SNE embedding to compare imputation methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction--32669ca88340d5c7))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when sample-wise small-RNA profiles (e.g. tncRNA sequence presence per tissue sample) need non-linear 2D embedding to test whether samples cluster by tissue of origin. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction--7c3aa54cf596667c))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when you have cells projected onto a small selected marker-gene panel (e.g. scGeneFit's 15 CBMC markers) plus cell type labels, and need a 2-D visualization to check that cell populations remain separated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction--b082838f8ce5c181))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction-of-cell-shape-latents**: Use when single-cell shape features are already encoded as autoencoder latent vectors (e.g., MIRIAM pipeline output) and you need a 2D t-SNE embedding to test whether similar geometries cluster and whether marker-positive cells form discrete populations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction-of-cell-shape-latents))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction-of-scrna-seq**: Use when you have normalized scRNA-seq count data (e.g. 10x Genomics PBMC, 4500 cells, 9 immune cell types) and need a 2D embedding to assess whether imputation or preprocessing improves subpopulation separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction-of-scrna-seq))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction-of-sequence-embeddings**: Use when you have per-sequence embeddings from a trained classifier checkpoint and need to test whether the representation space is taxonomically structured at a given rank (e.g., species or phylum). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction-of-sequence-embeddings))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when single-cell TLD predictions (or other per-cell text-like feature sets) must be visualized or clustered in 2D to test whether domain hierarchy carries cell-identity information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction-visualization))
- **tsne-dimensionality-reduction-visualization**: Use when you need to visually compare separability of predicted cancer/non-cancer driver gene classes across alternative graph-network embedding representations, especially for genes ranked by neighborhood discrete entropy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-dimensionality-reduction-visualization))
- **tsne-embedding-computation**: Use when benchmarking dimensionality-reduction scalability on a large scRNA-seq or CyTOF matrix (e.g. the 1.3M-cell 10X Genomics mouse brain dataset), or when a t-SNE baseline embedding is required for comparison against ivis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-computation))
- **tsne-embedding-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when when you have a precomputed similarity matrix of mass spectra (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-dimensionality-reduction) · 2 copies)
- **tsne-embedding-of-cell-similarity-matrices**: Use when you have cell-to-cell similarity matrices from scRNA-seq methods (e.g. SSRE vs Corr, SIMLR, MPSSC, SSR, native t-SNE) and need 2D embeddings to visually compare how well each separates known cell types/stages. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-cell-similarity-matrices))
- **tsne-embedding-of-eic-features**: Use when raw mzXML spectra from HPTLC-HRMS tracks have been converted into per-ion EIC features and a low-dimensional embedding is needed before clusterising ions of interest versus background. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-eic-features))
- **tsne-embedding-of-feature-distance-structure**: Use when you have a pairwise feature distance or similarity matrix (e.g. MS2 spectral similarity) and need a 2D visualization/coordinate table that preserves the original distance structure for downstream msFeaST dashboard networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-feature-distance-structure))
- **tsne-embedding-of-real-and-generated-cells**: Use when validating scGAN or cscGAN generated single-cell RNA-seq data: embeddings must show generated cells overlapping real validation clusters and recapitulating cluster/marker-gene structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-real-and-generated-cells))
- **tsne-embedding-of-scrna-seq-counts**: Use when you have a raw scRNA-seq count matrix (e.g., Tabula Muris FACS counts CSV) and need a 2D t-SNE embedding whose per-cell quality can be statistically assessed against a signal-less null. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-scrna-seq-counts))
- **tsne-embedding-of-similarity-matrices**: Use when you have MS/MS feature spectra (.mgf) and need a 2D overview layout of pairwise spectral similarities (e.g. ms2deepscore scores) to spot locally grouped high-similarity features before detailed network inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-similarity-matrices))
- **tsne-embedding-of-single-cell-data**: Use when single-cell expression matrices (e.g., Villani PBMC, Baron pancreatic islet) need a 2-D visualization of cell-type separation or batch mixing, or a t-SNE embedding is required before KMeans clustering for ASW/ARI/NMI/purity evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-single-cell-data--0958af159cbe895c))
- **tsne-embedding-of-single-cell-data**: Use when a high-dimensional single-cell feature matrix (e.g., latent representations from Miscell, Seurat, Scanpy, or scVI) needs a fast 2D embedding for visualization and downstream clustering on datasets up to ~55,656 cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-of-single-cell-data--0770bd9b279c109b))
- **tsne-embedding-on-similarity-matrix**: Use when a cell-to-cell similarity matrix (e.g. learned by SIMLR from scRNA-seq expression data) must be turned into low-dimensional coordinates for visualizing and interpreting subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-on-similarity-matrix))
- **tsne-embedding-quality-validation**: Use when generated or augmented scRNA-seq cells (e.g. from scGAN/cscGAN) must be checked for realism against held-out real cells before downstream analyses such as marker gene detection or classifier training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-quality-validation))
- **tsne-embedding-visualization**: Use when you have a metabolomics feature matrix with sample metadata and need to visually assess sample clustering, batch effects, or confounder-driven structure before classification modeling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-embedding-visualization--109e95bbb381db44))
- **tsne-embedding-visualization**: Use when you must build a structurally informed train/test split — e.g. testing how a spectral-prediction ranker generalizes to molecules dissimilar from its training set — by embedding binary fingerprints and grouping molecules into structural clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-embedding-visualization--a261fd48b278f506))
- **tsne-embedding-visualization**: Use when you have BUSseq-fitted corrected read counts (from corrected_read_counts) and need to validate that the batch-effect correction succeeded before downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-visualization--6fef696eed45e91e))
- **tsne-embedding-visualization**: Use when assessing whether simulated single-cell RNA-seq data (e.g., GRouNdGAN output vs 1000 held-out test cells) resembles the reference distribution, as the qualitative counterpart to metric-based evaluation in --evaluate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-visualization--ea3e062dd6a30771))
- **tsne-embedding-visualization**: Use when comparing batch-correction methods on multi-batch scRNA-seq matrices and a visual QC of batch mixing versus cell-type separation is needed to accompany quantitative metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-visualization--53af29bde1eecd3c))
- **tsne-embedding-visualization-of-single-cell-clusters**: Use when you have a log-transformed gene-cell-barcode matrix from tens of thousands of droplet-based 3' mRNA counting cells and need a 2D map that shows whether k-means clusters (e.g., k=10 on 50 PCs) form spatially distinct subpopulations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-embedding-visualization-of-single-cell-clusters))
- **tsne-latent-space-visualization**: Use when a learned single-cell latent embedding (e.g., from scMDC on CITE-seq RNA+ADT data) must be visually assessed for cell-type separation and batch-effect removal before/instead of relying solely on quantitative clustering metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-latent-space-visualization))
- **tsne-local-neighborhood-exploration**: Use when a precomputed pairwise MS/MS similarity matrix (e.g., ms2deepscore) and a .mgf feature set need interactive local exploration around a densely connected node to support structural hypothesis generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-local-neighborhood-exploration))
- **tsne-perplexity-grid-optimization**: Use when a Seurat scRNA-seq object (e.g., Tabula Muris marrow, CAR-T GSE125881, Hydra) needs an optimized t-SNE perplexity, or when comparing hyperparameter optimization by scDEED vs EMBEDR via dubious-embedding counts and KNN/KNC preservation metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-perplexity-grid-optimization))
- **tsne-perplexity-hyperparameter-sweep**: Use when a t-SNE (or UMAP) embedding's quality depends on an unknown scale hyperparameter and you need to pick perplexity/kEff or detect characteristic neighborhood scales using per-sample p values rather than visual inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-perplexity-hyperparameter-sweep))
- **tsne-perplexity-hyperparameter-verification**: Use when a single-cell count matrix has been embedded with t-SNE and you must verify or report how dubious/trustworthy embedding fractions change between an original and a scDEED-optimized perplexity setting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-perplexity-hyperparameter-verification))
- **tsne-perplexity-tuning**: Use when benchmarking a supervised or class-informed embedding (e.g. GraphDDP) against unsupervised baselines and you must demonstrate that t-SNE's perplexity choice can erase cluster/class structure on labeled single-cell data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-perplexity-tuning))
- **tsne-projection-of-bgc-embeddings**: Use when you have pfam2vec (or similar domain) embeddings and need to visually/quantitatively validate that BGCs of the same product subclass cluster together in the learned vector space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-projection-of-bgc-embeddings))
- **tsne-scatter-plot-visualization**: Use when you need to visually compare chemical-space coverage across multiple mass spectral libraries using normalized chemical identifiers, PubChem fingerprints, and t-SNE dimensionality reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/tsne-scatter-plot-visualization))
- **tsne-spurious-cluster-contrast**: Use when evaluating whether a dimensionality-reduction method artificially fragments homogeneous data into compact clusters, or when validating that global structure (cluster shapes and relative locations) survives 2D embedding of high-dimensional data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-spurious-cluster-contrast))
- **tsne-subcluster-verification**: Use when a spatial transcriptomics method assigns spots to a domain (e.g., region Y spanning tumor and connective tissue) and you must confirm internal heterogeneity, such as a reported sub-cluster count, before trusting the domain annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/tsne-subcluster-verification))
- **tsne-umap-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking**: Use when comparing dimensionality-reduction embeddings (scPhere vs t-SNE vs UMAP) on large scRNA-seq count matrices and you need a quantitative global-structure metric, e.g. k-NN accuracy to major cell types, rather than visual inspection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-umap-dimensionality-reduction-benchmarking))
- **tsne-umap-embedding-generation**: Use when a normalized/scaled scRNA-seq matrix with detected variable features already has PCA scores and you need 2D/3D cluster-visualizing cell projections (t-SNE and UMAP coordinates per cell). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-umap-embedding-generation))
- **tsne-umap-neighborhood-hyperparameter-optimization**: Use when comparing t-SNE and UMAP embedding quality at default (k=15) versus optimized neighborhood sizes on single-cell data, or when per-cell optimal perplexity/n_neighbors values are needed to distinguish real structure from algorithm-induced noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-umap-neighborhood-hyperparameter-optimization))
- **tsne-visualization-of-cell-clusters**: Use when an imputed or raw single-cell expression matrix must be visually inspected for cluster compactness and separation across cell types, e.g., alongside ARI/Jaccard/NMI/purity benchmarks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-visualization-of-cell-clusters))
- **tsne-visualization-of-latent-embeddings**: Use when a high-dimensional scRNA-seq embedding (e.g. a 20-dimensional scGAE latent space) must be rendered in 2D to visually verify cluster separation or resolve subpopulations before/after re-clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-visualization-of-latent-embeddings))
- **tsne-visualization-of-scrnaseq-latent-space**: Use when you have a low-dimensional per-cell representation (e.g., ZINB-WaVE W factors) and need a 2D t-SNE visualization of cell populations for clustering inspection or publication figures. - **tsne-visualization-of-simulated-counts**: Use when you have run SymSim's SimulateTrueCounts for discrete or continuous populations on a phyla tree (e.g. Phyla5()) and need to verify the simulated cell organization reflects the tree, or to inspect how Sigma changes population heterogeneity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-visualization-of-simulated-counts))
- **tsne-visualization-of-single-cell-profiles**: Use when you have translated or measured single-cell profiles (e.g., predicted ADT from consecutive epigenome→transcriptome→proteome translation) and need a 2-D embedding to visually assess cell-type separation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-visualization-of-single-cell-profiles))
- **tsne-visualization-single-cell**: Use when a normalized c-CSN-derived CNDM (m genes x n cells, e.g. &gt;20,000 Tabula Muris droplet cells) needs dimension reduction and 2D visualization to confirm that cells segregate by known origin or cluster structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tsne-visualization-single-cell))
- **tsnpe_hpr_truncated_proposal**: Compute TSNPE high-posterior-density truncation thresholds and rejection samples from a bounded prior. - **tsodyks-markram-chaotic-dynamics**: Tsodyks-Markram短时程突触可塑性的混沌动力学。研究确定性TM模型中Shilnikov同宿分岔导致混沌行为的路径，揭示网络动力学不可预测性和对初始条件的敏感性。适用于计算神经科学、突触可塑性建模、混沌动力学分析。触发词：短时程突触可塑性、Tsodyks-Markram模型、Shilnikov分岔、混沌动力学、short-term synaptic plasticity、Tsodyks-Markram model、Shilnikov homoclinic bifurcation、chaotic dynamics。 ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/control-systems/tsodyks-markram-chaotic-dynamics) · ★4 · 4 copies)

([alloc/skills](https://github.com/alloc/skills/tree/HEAD/frontend/tsrx-preact))

- **tss-and-cleavage-site-coordinate-anchoring**: Use when a motif enrichment analysis requires positional interpretation, i.e. you have assay-derived anchor coordinates (TSSs or cleavage sites) and want motif enrichment profiled at offsets around that anchor rather than presence/absence per sequence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/tss-and-cleavage-site-coordinate-anchoring))
- **tss-batch-prefetch**: Maintain the batched TSS-prefetch list of peripheral updaters (`PREFETCHABLE_UPDATERS` in `pymobiledevice3/restore/tss.py`). Use when onboarding a new device model, debugging a TSS rejection of the combined POST, or extending the prefetch to cover additional chips (e.g. Cryptex1, Timer, TCON, future Apple peripherals). Walks through enumerating candidates from `PreflightInfo.DeviceInfo`, adding entries safely, and validating with a non-destructive dry-run before a live restore. ([doronz88/pymobiledevice3](https://github.com/doronz88/pymobiledevice3/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/tss-batch-prefetch) · ★3.1k)
- **tss-coordinate-sequence-extraction**: Use when you have a set of TSS coordinates (e.g. EPDnew, GSE221852 csRNA-seq, GSE89299 RAMPAGE) and need promoter windows around each TSS as FASTA for motif-position analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tss-coordinate-sequence-extraction))
- **tss-dataset-quality-assessment**: Use when you have a set of TSS annotations (e.g. from EPDnew/CAGE, csRNA-seq GSE221852, or RAMPAGE GSE89299) and want to judge dataset quality or compare TSS sources by the stringency of core promoter element positioning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tss-dataset-quality-assessment))
- **tss-enrichment-cell-filtering**: Use when starting a scATAC-seq analysis from a fragments file (e.g. BMMC data) and you must exclude low-quality cells — low transcription start site (TSS) enrichment or too few unique nuclear fragments — before building the peak-count matrix and running LSI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tss-enrichment-cell-filtering))
- **tss-proximal-peak-aggregation**: Use when a mosaic dataset contains scATAC-seq batches but lacks scRNA-seq matrices for some batches (None placeholders in the counts dictionary), so that gene activity scores can substitute for missing gene expression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/tss-proximal-peak-aggregation))
- **tss-tts-distance-normalization**: Use when profiling how far UMI-collapsed transcript coverage extends from transcript ends in 10x Chromium scRNA-seq data (e.g., to justify detecting SNVs up to ~10 kbp from the capture site).
